To conduct a further estimation of
\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|
, we analyze lower and upper bounds for the denominator and the numerator above. We start with the denominator first

\displaystyle\delta^{(j)}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{r}(A^{(j)})-\sigma_{r+1}(\widetilde{A}^{(j)})

\displaystyle=\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})-\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}+\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}).

Using properties of singular values of the matrix product for the first term above and Weyl’s bound for the second term, we get

\displaystyle\delta^{(j)}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}\Big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)-\sigma_{r+1}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)-\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}\Big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}\Big)-\sigma_{r+1}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)-\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|.

(D.18)

For the norm of
C^{(j)}
,
the following upper bound can be established

\displaystyle\|C^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|+\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|

(D.19)

Now, combining bounds (
D.18
) and (
D.19
), one obtains that

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\alpha\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}{\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}\big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}\big)-\sigma_{r+1}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)-\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}:

Using in the denominator the fact that
\sigma_{r+1}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)\leq\alpha\sigma_{r+1}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\alpha\sigma_{r+1}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|
finishes the proof of this lemma.
∎

Further, we consider the gradient descent iterates with its t-SVD

{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}=\sum_{s=1}^{R}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}}(:,s,:)*\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}}(s,s,:)*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}}^{\top}(:,s,:)}

and the corresponding Fourier domain representation
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu=\operatorname{diag}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(1)},\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(2)},\ldots,\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(k)})
, where
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{R}\sigma_{\ell}^{(j)}v_{\ell}^{(j)}{w_{\ell}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}=V_{U_{t}}^{(j)}\Sigma_{U_{t}}^{(j)}W_{U_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}
and its signal-noise term decomposition

\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}=\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}+\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}

We also define the corresponding new tensors

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=1}^{r}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}}(:,s,:)*\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}}(s,s,:)*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{\top}(:,s,:)

(D.20)

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=r+1}^{R}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}}(:,s,:)*\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}}(s,s,:)*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}}^{\top}(:,s,:)

(D.21)

and their Fourier domain representations

\displaystyle\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu

\displaystyle=\operatorname{diag}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(1)},\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(2)},\ldots,\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(k)}),\quad\quad{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\sigma_{\ell}^{(j)}v_{\ell}^{(j)}{w_{\ell}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}=V_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}\Sigma_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}W_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}}

(D.22)

\displaystyle\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu

\displaystyle=\operatorname{diag}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muN_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(1)},\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muN_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(2)},\ldots,\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muN_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(k)}),\quad\quad{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muN_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\sum_{\ell=r+1}^{R}\sigma_{\ell}^{(j)}v_{\ell}^{(j)}{w_{\ell}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}=V_{\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}}^{(j)}\Sigma_{\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}}^{(j)}W_{\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}}

(D.23)

Lemma D.4
.

Assume
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{1}{2}
. Then it holds that

\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|\leq 2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\sigma_{r+1}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)}{\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|.

(D.24)

Proof.

Consider
\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{\mathrm{T}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|
.
For each
1\leq j\leq k
, we can now exploit the results in
[
36
, Lemma A.1]
, to get that

\|(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{\top}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\frac{\|\Sigma_{\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{min}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)}\quad\text{and}\quad\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\geq\frac{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{2}.

From here, we have we can proceed as follows

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq 2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\|\Sigma_{\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}

\displaystyle=2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{N}}_{t}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\leq 2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\|

\displaystyle=2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\sigma_{r+1}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)}{\sigma_{r}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|,

which concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma D.5
.

Assume that
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{1}{8}
for some
t\geq 1,t\in\mathbb{N}
. Then for each
1\leq j\leq k
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)

(D.25)

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}).

(D.26)

Moreover, the principal angles between the tensor-column subspaces spanned by
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
and
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
can be estimated as follows

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 7\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|

(D.27)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}).

(D.28)

Proof.

We assume that
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{1}{8}
, then due to Lemma
D.4
, we obtain that

\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|\leq 2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\sigma_{r+1}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{j}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)}{\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{j}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{1}{4}.

(D.29)

Now, to estimate
\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)
, we see that for each
1\leq j\leq k
, it holds that

\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)^{2}=\sigma_{r}\Big(\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)=\sigma_{r}\Big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)\\

(D.30)

Since
{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}={\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}+{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muN_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muN_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
, we get that

\displaystyle\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r}\Big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)=\sigma_{r}\big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r}\big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}W_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\big)^{2}\sigma_{r}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)^{2}\geq(1-\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{T}\|^{2}\big)\sigma_{r}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)^{2},

where in the last line we used the definition of the principal angle between tensor column subspaces and the corresponding properties in their Fourier domain slices, namely

\sigma_{r}\big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}W_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\big)^{2}=1-\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}W_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\perp}\|^{2}\geq 1-\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}W_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\perp}\|^{2}=1-\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{T}\|^{2}.

Due to our assumption
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{1}{8}
, we can see that in the Fourier domain, the subspaces spanned by
{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}
and
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}=V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}
are close enough. Then, decomposing
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
into two different ways, namely as

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{R}\sigma_{\ell}^{(j)}v_{\ell}^{(j)}{w_{\ell}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muL_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}+\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muN_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}

and as

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}+\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}},

according to Lemma
H.1
, one obtains for each
1\leq j\leq k
that

\displaystyle\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 7\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}),

where the last inequality is equivalent to
\sigma_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\leq 2\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})
. According to the definition of principal angles between tensor subspaces, this implies that

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|=\max_{j}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\|\leq 7\max_{j}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{t}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}\|=7\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|.

In the same way,

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|=\max_{j}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq 2\max_{j}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})
, which finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma D.6
.

Consider a tensor
\bm{\mathcal{T}}:=\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\in S^{n\times n\times k}_{+}
with tubal rank
r\leq n
. Assume that measurement operator
\mathcal{A}
is such that

\bm{\mathcal{M}}=\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{T}})=\bm{\mathcal{T}}+\bm{\mathcal{E}}\quad\in S^{n\times n\times k}_{+}

and for for each
1\leq j\leq k
one has
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\delta\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{T}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})
with
\delta\leq\frac{1}{4}
.
For the same
\bm{\mathcal{M}}
with its t-SVD
\bm{\mathcal{M}}=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}*\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}^{\top}
, let
\bm{\mathcal{L}}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r\times k}
denote the tensor column subspace spanned by the tensor-columns corresponding to the first
r
singular tubes, that is
\bm{\mathcal{L}}:=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}(:,1:r,:)\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r\times k}
.

Then, in each Fourier slice
j
,
1\leq j\leq k
, it holds that

\displaystyle(1-\delta)\lambda_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\leq(1+\delta)\lambda_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

(D.31)

\displaystyle\lambda_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq\delta\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

(D.32)

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\geq(1-\delta)\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}),

(D.33)

and

(1-\delta)\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\leq(1+\delta)\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}\|

(D.34)

Moreover, the tensor-column subspaces of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
and
\bm{\mathcal{L}}
are aligned, namely

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\|\leq 2\delta

(D.35)

Proof.

Consider tensor
\bm{\mathcal{T}}:=\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\in S^{n\times n\times k}_{+}
. Due to the definition of tensor transpose and conjugate
symmetry of Fourier coefficients
[
17
]
, the Fourier slices of
\bm{\mathcal{T}}
are defined as
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}
. That is, each face
of
\bm{\mathcal{T}}
is Hermitian and at least positive semidefinite.
As we assume that for each
j
,
1\leq j\leq k
, one has
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\delta\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{T}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})
using Weyl’s inequality in each of the Fourier slices, we obtain the first three inequalities.

To show that the tensor subspace
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}
and
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}
are aligned, we use first use the definition

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\|=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}\|

(D.36)

For the estimation of
\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}\|
in each of the Fourier slices, we apply Wedin’s
\sin\Theta
theorem.
For this, denote
\bm{\mathcal{L}}:=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}(:,1:r,:)\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r\times k}
and let
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}
denote the corresponding Fourier slices of
\bm{\mathcal{L}}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r\times k}
.
Since in the Fourier space, it holds that
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}+\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
and
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}
encompasses the first
r
eigenvectors of
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
, from Wedin’s
\sin\Theta
theorem, we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}\|\leq\frac{\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|}{\xi^{(j)}},

with
\xi^{(j)}:=\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})-\lambda_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})
. Using estimate (
D.32
),
\xi^{(j)}
can be lower-bounded as

\xi^{(j)}:=\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})-\lambda_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\geq\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})-\delta\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})=(1-\delta)\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}).

Using the bound the the assumptions that
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\delta\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{T}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})
and
\delta\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we get

\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}\|\leq\frac{\delta}{1-\delta}\leq 2\delta.

Coming back to equality (
D.36
), we obtain the stated bound for the principal angle between the two tensor column subspaces.
∎

Lemma D.7
.

Consider a tensor
\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\in S^{n\times n\times k}_{+}
with tubal rank
r\leq n
. Assume that measurement operator
\mathcal{A}
is such that

\bm{\mathcal{M}}=\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top})=\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}+\bm{\mathcal{E}}

and for each,
j
,
1\leq j\leq k
, one has
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\delta\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}})
with
\delta\leq c_{1}
. Moreover, assume that for difference tensor
\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}=\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}-\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{t}
it holds that

\gamma:=\frac{\alpha\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}{\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\frac{1}{\alpha\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2},

(D.37)

where
c_{1},c_{2}>0
are sufficiently small absolute constants. Then for the signal and noise term of the gradient descent (
C.1
), we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 14(\delta+\gamma)

(D.38)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

(D.39)

and for each
j
,
1\leq j\leq k
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4}\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

(D.40)

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

(D.41)

Proof.

To prove the above-stated properties, we will use Lemma
D.3
. Therefore, we start by checking the conditions of this lemma. Sufficiently small
c_{2}
and the assumption
\gamma\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2}
allows for
\gamma\leq\frac{1}{2}
. This means that

\frac{\alpha\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}{\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\frac{1}{\alpha\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\leq\frac{1}{2}

and in each of the Fourier slices we have

\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}{\alpha}\leq\frac{1}{2}{\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu),

fulfilling the assumption of Lemma
D.3
. Hence, from Lemma
D.3
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\alpha\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}{\alpha\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}\big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu*\bm{\mathcal{U}}}\big)-\alpha\sigma_{r+1}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|-\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}

(D.42)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}{\alpha\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}\big({\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu*\bm{\mathcal{U}}}\big)-\alpha\displaystyle\max_{1\leq jlek}\sigma_{r+1}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|-\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|},

(D.43)

and, moreover, together with Lemma
D.5
and the assumption
\gamma\leq\frac{1}{2}
we get

\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\geq\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)-\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|\geq\frac{\alpha}{2}\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

(D.44)

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|\leq\alpha\gamma\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

(D.45)

The last two inequalities, allow extend bound (
D.42
) as follows

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{\gamma}{1-\gamma}

(D.46)

Now, consider the principal angle between
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
and
\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}
using its definition

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}\|=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\|\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\|+\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\|+\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\|

\displaystyle=\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|

Using the last line above, and inequalities (
D.35
) and (
D.46
), we obtain

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq 2(\delta+\gamma).

From here, allowing
\delta
and
\gamma
to be such that
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{1}{8}
, we can use Lemma
D.5
to get

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq 7\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}_{t}}\|\leq 14(\delta+\gamma).

Furthermore, Lemma
D.5
together with inequality (
D.45
) also results in

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq 2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

and for the spectral norm of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}
we get

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|\leq 2\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\\
\leq\frac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu).

To conclude the proof, we see that Lemma
D.5
together with inequality (
D.44
) provides for each
j
,
1\leq j\leq k
, the following lower bound

\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)\geq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)\geq\frac{\alpha}{4}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\geq\frac{\alpha}{4}\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu).

∎

The following lemma shows that for an appropriately chosen initialization, in the first new iteration, the tensor column subspaces between the signal term
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
and the ground truth tensor
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
become aligned. Moreover, for each
1\leq j\leq k
there is a solid gap between the smallest singular values of the signal term and the largest singular values of the noise term.

Lemma D.8
.

Assume
\mathcal{A}:S^{n\times n\times k}\to\mathbb{R}^{m}
satisfies the
\text{S2NRIP}(\delta_{1})
for some constant
\delta_{1}>0
. Also, assume that

\bm{\mathcal{M}}:=\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top})=\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}+\bm{\mathcal{E}}

with
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\delta\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}})
for each
1\leq j\leq k
and
\delta\leq c_{1}\kappa^{-2}
.

Denote by
\bm{\mathcal{L}}
the tensor-columns corresponding to the first
r
singular tubes in the t-SVD of
\bm{\mathcal{M}}
, that is,
\bm{\mathcal{L}}:=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}(:,1:r,:)\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r\times k}
, and
define the initialization
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}=\alpha\bm{\mathcal{U}}
with the coefficient
\alpha
such that

\alpha^{2}\leq\frac{c\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}{12k\sqrt{\min\{n,R\}}\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{-48\kappa^{2}}\min{\{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu),\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}\}

(D.47)

where
v_{1}\in\mathbb{C}^{nk}
is the leading eigenvector of matrix
{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{M}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\in\mathbb{C}^{nk\times nk}}
.

Assume that learning rate
\mu
fulfils
\mu\leq c_{3}\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}
, then after
t_{\star}
iterations with

t_{\star}\asymp\frac{1}{\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}}\ln\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)

(D.48)

it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

(D.49)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star}}}\|

\displaystyle\leq c\kappa^{-2}.

(D.50)

and for each
1\leq j\leq k
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4}\alpha\beta

(D.51)

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star},\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\beta

(D.52)

where
\beta
satisfies
\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\leq\beta\leq\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{16\kappa^{2}}
.

Proof.

For the proof of this lemma, we want to apply Lemma
D.7
. The first condition of Lemma
D.7
is the following

\gamma:=\frac{\alpha\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|}{\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\frac{1}{\alpha\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2},

By the definition of
\gamma
, it is sufficient to show that

\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|\leq\frac{c_{3}}{2\kappa^{2}}\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

(D.54)

and

\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|\leq\frac{c_{3}}{2\kappa^{2}}\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu).

(D.55)

Since for
\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{t}=(\bm{\mathcal{I}}+\mu\bm{\mathcal{M}})^{*t}
the transformation in the Fourier domain leads to the blocks

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t}=(\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{t},

this means that inequality (
D.54
) is equivalent to

\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\leq\left(\frac{1+\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)^{t},

which can be further modified as

\ln\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)\leq t\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right).

Hence, if we take
t_{\star}
as follows

t_{\star}:=\left\lceil\ln\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)\middle/\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)\right\rceil

(D.56)

then condition (
D.54
) will be satisfied in each block in the Fourier domain. For convenience, we will further denote

\psi:=\ln\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right).

(D.57)

For the second part of Lemma
D.7
’s condition, inequality (
D.55
), we will use Lemma
D.1
. To apply this Lemma, the condition
t_{\star}\leq t^{\star}
needs to be satisfied.
According to Lemma
D.2

t^{\star}\geq\left\lfloor\frac{\ln{\left(\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\cdot\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}}{8(1+\delta_{1}\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k\min{\{n,R\}}}\alpha^{3}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}\right)}}{2\ln{\left(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\right)}}\right\rfloor

(D.58)

For
t_{\star}\leq t^{\star}
to hold, it will be sufficient to check, e.g., the following condition

\frac{\psi}{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)}\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot\frac{\ln{\left(\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\cdot\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}}{8(1+\delta_{1}\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k\min{\{n,R\}}}\alpha^{3}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}\right)}}{2\ln{\left(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\right)}}.

To check this condition let us first analyze the expression
{\ln{\left(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\right)}}/{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)}
first.
Using
\frac{x}{1+x}\leq\ln(1+x)\leq x
, we can upper bound the above expression as

\frac{\ln{\left(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\right)}}{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)}\leq\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|(1+\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}))}{\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})-\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}

(D.59)

From here, applying the PSD of the tensor representatives in the Fourier domain and the assumptions
\delta\leq\frac{1}{3}
and
\mu\leq c_{3}\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}
and Lemma
D.6
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|(1+\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}))}{\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})-\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(1+\delta)\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}\|}{(1-2\delta)\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\left(1+c_{3}(1+\delta)\left(\frac{\lambda_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{\kappa\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa^{2}\frac{(1+\delta)}{(1-2\delta)}(1+c_{3}(1+\delta))\leq 8\kappa^{2},

in the last line, we used the bound on
\delta
and that
c_{3}
can be taken small enough.
This means

\frac{\ln{\left(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\right)}}{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)}\leq 8\kappa^{2}.

(D.60)

Thus, to show that
t_{\star}\leq t^{\star}
, it is sufficient to tune the initialization factor
\alpha
so that

\psi\cdot 32\kappa^{2}\leq\ln{\left(\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\cdot\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}}{8(1+\delta_{1}\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k\min{\{n,R\}}}\alpha^{3}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}\right)}.

or using the notation for
\phi
, this is equivalent to

\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{32\kappa^{2}}\leq\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\cdot\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}}{8(1+\delta_{1}\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k\min{\{n,R\}}}\alpha^{3}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}

Since
\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}/\alpha=\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}
, The last inequality is implied if

\alpha^{2}\leq\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{-32\kappa^{2}}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\cdot\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}}{8(1+\delta_{1}\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k\min{\{n,R\}}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}},

or if we set
\alpha
even smaller using the fact that
(1+\delta_{1}\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k}\leq(1+\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k}\leq 2k
and
\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\geq\frac{2}{3}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}
and set the parameter
\alpha
so that

\alpha^{2}\leq\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{-32\kappa^{2}}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\cdot\|{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu}^{\mathrm{H}}v_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}}{24k\sqrt{\min{\{n,R\}}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}.

Hence
t_{\star}\leq t^{\star}
is satisfied and applying Lemma
D.7
, we get

\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t_{\star}}\|\leq 8(1+\delta_{1}\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k\min{\{n,R\}}}\frac{\alpha^{3}}{\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{3t_{\star}}

(D.61)

Moreover, using
\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\geq\tfrac{2}{3}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}
from Lemma
D.6
with
\delta\leq 1/3
and
(1+\delta_{1}\sqrt{k})\sqrt{k}\leq 2k
, we get

\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t_{\star}}\|\leq 12k\sqrt{\min{\{n,R\}}}\frac{\alpha^{3}}{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{3t_{\star}}

Hence, using that
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=(\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{t}
inequality (
D.55
) will be implied if

12k\sqrt{\min{\{n,R\}}}\frac{\alpha^{3}}{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{3t_{\star}}\leq\frac{c_{3}}{2\kappa^{2}}\alpha\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}\Big((\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{t_{\star}}\Big)\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu),

which is equivalent to

\alpha^{2}\leq c_{3}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{12k\sqrt{\min{\{n,R\}}}\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}\frac{(1+\mu\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}))^{t_{\star}}}{(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{3t_{\star}}},

(D.62)

for all
j
. To proceed further, let us analyze the last factor from above using the definition of
t_{\star}
. Note that

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\mu\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}))^{t_{\star}}}{(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{3t_{\star}}}

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(t_{\star}\ln{\left(\frac{1+\mu\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{3}}\right)}\right)}\geq\exp{\left(-3t_{\star}\ln{\left({(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{3}}\right)}\right)}

Now, using the definition of
t_{\star}
, that is
t_{\star}=\left\lceil\psi/\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}{1+\mu\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)\right\rceil
and inequality (
D.60
), we get

\exp{\left(-3t_{\star}\ln{\Big({(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{3}}\Big)}\right)}\geq\exp{\left(-48\psi\kappa^{2}\right)}=\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{-48\kappa^{2}}

(D.63)

Inserting this into inequality (
D.62
), we get

\alpha^{2}\leq c_{3}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{12\,k\sqrt{\min{\{n,R\}}}\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{3}}\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{-48\kappa^{2}}.

(D.64)

For such
\alpha
, we have shown that inequality (
D.55
) holds, and the condition of Lemma
D.7
is fulfilled, which gives us

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 14(\delta+\gamma)\leq c\kappa^{-2},

(D.65)

where the last inequality follows from our assumption that
\delta\leq c_{1}\kappa^{-2}
and
\mu\leq c_{3}\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}
and from setting the constants
c_{1}
and
c_{3}
small enough.

Moreover, for each
1\leq j\leq k
, from Lemma
D.7
it follows that

\displaystyle\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4}\alpha\beta,

(D.66)

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\beta.

(D.67)

where
\beta:={\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)
.

In the remaining part, we will show that
t_{\star}
,
\beta
and
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}\|
have the properties stated in the lemma.

Let us start with
t_{\star}
. Using the same inequalities for
\ln(1+x)
as above and Lemma
D.6
, one can show

{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)\geq\frac{\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}-\mu\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\geq\frac{2}{3}\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}}

and at the same time

{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)}\leq\ln\left(1+\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\right)\leq\mu\displaystyle\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})

\leq\mu(1+\delta)\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}\leq 4/3\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}

which shows that, on the one hand,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)}\leq\frac{2}{3\mu}\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{1}{\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}}=\frac{2}{3\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}}

and on the other hand

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)}\geq\frac{3}{4\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}},

which shows the desired properties of
t_{\star}
.

Now, we consider
\beta:=\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t_{\star}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)
.
By the definition of
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
and inequality (
D.60
), we get

\displaystyle\Big(1+\mu\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\Big)^{t_{\star}}

\displaystyle=\exp\Big(t_{\star}\ln(1+\mu\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}))\Big)\leq\exp\Big(t_{\star}\ln(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(2\psi\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\frac{\ln\left(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|\right)}{\ln\left(\frac{1+\mu\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}{1+\mu\sigma_{r+1}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muM\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}\right)}\right)\leq\exp(16\psi\kappa^{2})=\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}16}\kappa^{2}}.

(D.68)

Since this holds for all
j
, we have

\beta\leq\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{16\kappa^{2}}.

Finally, we come to the properties of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t^{\star}}
. By the representation
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}=\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}+\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t_{\star}}
, we get

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t^{\star}}\|\leq\alpha\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{t_{\star}}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t_{\star}}\|.

From (
D.55
), we get

\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\|\leq\frac{c_{3}}{2\kappa^{2}}\alpha\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{t}\|\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\leq\frac{c_{3}}{2\kappa^{2}}\alpha\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{t}\|\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{\mathrm{H}})\sigma_{max}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\leq\alpha\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{t}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|,

which allows us to proceed as follows

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t^{\star}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{t_{\star}}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|\leq 2\alpha(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)^{t_{\star}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|,

\displaystyle=2\alpha\ln\Big(t_{\star}(1+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|)\Big)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|\leq 2\alpha\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|\left({\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}}\right)^{16\kappa^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|\sqrt{\frac{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{12\,k\sqrt{\min{\{n,R\}}}\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}}\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|}{c_{3}\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\right)^{-8\kappa^{2}}\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|,

where for the second inequality above we used (
D.68
) and in the last one an upper bound on
\alpha
from (
D.64
) has been applied.

∎

The results in Lemma
D.8
hold for any initialization
\bm{\mathcal{U}}
. Below, we will use the fact that
\bm{\mathcal{U}}
is a tensor with Gaussian entries. This yields the following lemma, which shows with initialization scale
\alpha>0
be chosen sufficiently small, the properties stated in Lemma
D.8
hold with high probability.

Lemma D.9
.

Fix a sufficiently small constant
c>0
. Let
\bm{\mathcal{U}}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times R\times k}
be a random tubal tensor with i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,\tfrac{1}{R})
entries, and let
\epsilon\in(0,1)
. Assume that
\mathcal{A}:S^{n\times n\times k}\to\mathbb{R}^{m}
satisfies the
\text{S2NRIP}(\delta_{1})
for some constant
\delta_{1}>0
. Also, assume that

\bm{\mathcal{M}}:=\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top})=\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}+\bm{\mathcal{E}}

with
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muE\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\leq\delta\lambda_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}})
for each
1\leq j\leq k
, where
\delta\leq c_{1}\kappa^{-2}
. Let
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{0}=\alpha\bm{\mathcal{U}}
where

\alpha^{2}\lesssim\begin{cases}\dfrac{\epsilon\min\{n,R\}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}{k^{2}n^{3/2}\kappa^{2}}\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}kn^{3/2}}{c_{3}\min\{n,R\}^{3/2}\epsilon}\right)^{-24\kappa^{2}}&\text{if }R\geq 3r\\
\dfrac{\epsilon\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}{k^{2}n^{3/2}\kappa^{2}}\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}kn^{3/2}}{c_{3}r^{1/2}\epsilon}\right)^{-24\kappa^{2}}&\text{if }R<3r\end{cases}.

Assume the step size satisfies
\mu\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}
. Then, with probability at least
1-p
where

p=\begin{cases}k(\tilde{C}\epsilon)^{R-r+1}+ke^{-\tilde{c}R}&\text{if }R\geq 2r\\
k\epsilon^{2}+ke^{-\tilde{c}R}&\text{if }R<2r\end{cases}

the following statement holds. After

t_{\star}\lesssim\begin{cases}\dfrac{1}{\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}}\ln\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{c_{3}\epsilon\sqrt{\min\{n;R\}}}\right)&\text{if }R\geq 3r\\
\frac{1}{\mu\min_{1\leq j\leq k}\sigma_{r}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})^{2}}\ln\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rn}}{c_{3}\epsilon}\right)&\text{if }R<3r\end{cases}

iterations, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

(D.69)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star}}}\|

\displaystyle\leq c\kappa^{-2}.

(D.70)

and for each
1\leq j\leq k
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4}\alpha\beta

(D.71)

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star},\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\beta

(D.72)

where

\beta\lesssim\begin{cases}\epsilon\sqrt{k}\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{c_{3}\epsilon\sqrt{\min\{n;R\}}}\right)^{16\kappa^{2}}&\text{if }R\geq 3r\\
\dfrac{\epsilon\sqrt{k}}{r}\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rn}}{c_{3}\epsilon}\right)^{16\kappa^{2}}&\text{if }R<3r\end{cases}

and

\beta\gtrsim\begin{cases}\epsilon\sqrt{k}&\text{if }R\geq 3r\\
\dfrac{\epsilon\sqrt{k}}{r}&\text{if }R<3r\end{cases}.

Proof.

By Lemma
I.3
, we have that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\dfrac{k\max\{n,R\}}{R}}=\sqrt{\dfrac{kn}{\min\{n;R\}}}
with probability at least
1-O(ke^{-c\max\{n,R\}})
. Also, by Lemma
I.4
, we have that
\|\overline{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}^{H}\bm{v}_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}=\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1}\|_{F}\asymp\sqrt{k}
with probability at least
1-O(ke^{-cR})
. Since
\bm{\mathcal{U}}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times R\times k}
has i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,\tfrac{1}{R})
entries and
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}=\bm{\mathcal{I}}
, by rotational invariance,
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times R\times k}
also has i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,\tfrac{1}{R})
entries. Hence, the lower bound on
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}})
in Lemma
I.2
applies. If
r\leq R\leq 2r
, we have

\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}})\geq\dfrac{\epsilon\sqrt{k}}{\sqrt{rR}}\gtrsim\dfrac{\epsilon\sqrt{k}}{r}

with probability at least
1-k\epsilon^{2}
. If
2r<R<3r
, we have

\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}})\geq\dfrac{\epsilon\sqrt{k}(\sqrt{R}-\sqrt{2r-1})}{\sqrt{R}}\geq\dfrac{\epsilon\sqrt{k}(R-(2r-1))}{\sqrt{r}(\sqrt{R}+\sqrt{2r-1})}\gtrsim\dfrac{\epsilon\sqrt{k}}{r}

with probability at least
1-k(C\epsilon)^{R-2r+1}-ke^{-cR}
. If
R\geq 3r
, we have

\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}})\geq\dfrac{\epsilon\sqrt{k}(\sqrt{R}-\sqrt{2r-1})}{\sqrt{R}}=\epsilon\sqrt{k}\left(1-\sqrt{\tfrac{2r-1}{R}}\right)\gtrsim\epsilon\sqrt{k}

with probability at least
1-k(C\epsilon)^{R-2r+1}-ke^{-cR}
.

Therefore, the above bounds on
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|
,
\|\overline{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}^{H}\bm{v}_{1}\|_{\ell_{2}}
, and
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{L}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}})
all hold simultaneously with probability at least
1-p
where

p=\begin{cases}k(\tilde{C}\epsilon)^{R-r+1}+ke^{-\tilde{c}R}&\text{if }R\geq 2r\\
k\epsilon^{2}+ke^{-\tilde{c}R}&\text{if }R<2r\end{cases}.

Provided that all three of these bounds hold, one can substitute these into Lemma
D.8
to obtain the desired result.
∎

Appendix E
Analysis of convergence stage

In this section, we will prove that after passing the spectral stage,
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
goes into the convergence process towards the ground truth tensor
\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}
in the Frobenius norm.
For this, we will first show that in each of the tensor slices
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})
grows exponentially, see Lemma
E.1
, whereas the noise terms
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|
,
1\leq j\leq k
, grow slower, see Lemma
E.3
. Moreover, in Lemma
E.5
, we show the tensor column spaces of the signal term
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
and
the ground truth
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
stay aligned. With this, and several auxiliary lemmas in place, we show that

Lemma E.1
.

Assume that the following conditions hold

\displaystyle\mu

\displaystyle\leq c\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\kappa^{-2}

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq c\kappa^{-1}

and

\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\leq c\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu).

(E.1)

Moreover, assume that
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}
has full tubal rank with all invertible t-SVD-singular tubes.
Then, for each
j
,
1\leq j\leq k
, it holds that

\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\geq\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\geq\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\Big(1+\frac{1}{4}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)-\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\Big).

Proof.

Consider the tensor
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
. Using the definition of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}
in terms of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}
, we can rewrite it as

\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\big(\mathcal{I}+\mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\big)*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}.

This representation leads to the following representation of the RHS above in the Fourier domain

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}(\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\big)\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}:=H^{(j)}.

Note that here
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
can not be represented as an independent slice of measurements of
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}
as it involved the information about all the slices
1\leq j\leq k
.

Due to our assumptions on
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|
and the tensor spectral norm property, we get

\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|.

This in turn is leading to

\mu\leq c\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\kappa^{-2}\leq\tilde{c}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{-2}.

This property of
\mu
together with the nature of
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}
and
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)}
coming along from the signal-noise-term decomposition (
C.1
) leads to the fulfilled conditions of Lemma
H.2
. Applying Lemma
H.2
to the matrix
H^{(j)}
,
the smallest singular value of matrix
H^{(j)}
can be estimated as

\sigma_{min}(H^{(j)})\geq\big(1+\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})-\mu\|P_{1}^{(j)}\|-\mu\|P_{2}^{(j)}\|-\mu^{2}\|P_{3}^{(j)}\|\big)\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big(1-\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big).

(E.2)

with

\displaystyle\|P_{1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\|P_{2}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\left\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}+\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\|P_{3}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}.

Further, we will make the above bounds for
\|P_{i}^{(j)}\|,i\in\{1,2,3\},
more precise using information about the tensor setting.

First of all since
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
, we get
\|P_{1}^{(j)}\|\leq 36\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|^{2}
.
Moreover, since
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
and
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq c\kappa^{-1}
due to the assumption, it follows that for each
j,\;1\leq j\leq k
, it holds that
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\leq c\kappa^{-1}
. This allows for the following estimation

\|P_{1}^{(j)}\|\leq 36\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}c\kappa^{-1}\leq\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu),

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
c>0
is small enough.

Before proceeding with
\|P_{2}^{(j)}\|
, consider

(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})=(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})-\big(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\big).

The RHS from above has the following slices in the Fourier domain

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}-\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\big),

the norm of which (due to assumption (
E.1
) and the definition of the tensor spectral norm) can be bounded as
