\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}-\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\big)\|\leq\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\leq c\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu).

This leads to the following estimation

\|P_{2}^{(j)}\|\leq 4c\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

To further assess
\|P_{3}^{(j)}\|
, we take into account that matrix
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}
is an orthogonal matrix and the assumption
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
, which allows for the next bound

\|P_{3}^{(j)}\|\leq 2\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}\leq 2\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}\leq 2\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2}\leq 18\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{4}.

Inserting the newly obtained estimates for
\|P_{i}^{(j)}\|,i\in\{1,2,3\},
into (
E.2
),
we get

\displaystyle\sigma_{min}(H^{(j)})

\displaystyle\geq(1+\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)-4\mu c\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)-18\mu^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{4})\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\cdot\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big(1-\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\geq(1+\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)-4\mu c\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)-18\mu^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{4})\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big(1-\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big).

Now, according to the assumption on
\mu
, we get

\mu^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{4}\leq\mu c\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{4}=\mu c\frac{\sigma^{2}_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{4}=c\mu\sigma^{2}_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

Taking
c
small enough allows for the following estimation

\displaystyle\sigma_{min}(H^{(j)})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{(j)\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big(1+\frac{1}{2}\mu\sigma^{2}_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\big)\big(1-\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\Big(1+\frac{1}{2}\mu\sigma^{2}_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\big(1-\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\big)-\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\Big)

Now, since
{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\leq\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}
, we have that

{\mu\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\,\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t})\leq\mu 9\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\leq 9c\kappa^{-2}\leq\frac{1}{2}}

due to the fact that
{c>0}
can be chosen small enough.
The last part of Lemma’s proof follows from
{\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\geq\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})}
and
\sigma_{min}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})=\sigma_{min}(H^{(j)})
, which completes the argument.
∎

The next two lemmas will allow us to show that in each of the Fourier slices the noise term part of the gradient descent iterates is growing slower than its signal term part.

Lemma E.2
.

Assume that
{\mu\leq c\min\left\{\tfrac{1}{10}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2},\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|^{-1}\right\}}
and
{\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}
.
Moreover, suppose that
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}
has full tubal rank with all invertible t-SVD-tubes and
{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq c\kappa^{-1}}
with a sufficiently small contact
c>0
.
Then, the principal angle between
{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}}
and
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}
can be bounded as follows

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq 2\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+2\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|.

In particular, it holds that
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq 1/50
.

Proof.

By the definition of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}
, we have

\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}=\Big(\mathcal{I}+\mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\Big)*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\quad\quad\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r\times k},

which allows for the following representation in the Fourier domain

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\left(\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\right)\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\quad\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times r},\quad 1\leq j\leq k.

Consider the SVD decomposition
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\Sigma_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}W_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}
and denote by
Z^{(j)}
the matrix

Z^{(j)}:=\left(\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\right)V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\quad\quad\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times r}.

Since by assumption
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
has full rank (due to full-rankness of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}
, see Lemma
C.1
), matrix
Z^{(j)}
has the same column space as
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
and the principal angle between tensor subspaces
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}
and
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}
can be computed via
Z^{(j)}
as

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\|=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\|=\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{Z^{(j)}}\|.

Now, we will consider each of the terms
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{Z^{(j)}}\|
separately and bound them as follows

\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{Z^{(j)}}\|\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{Z^{(j)}}\Sigma_{Z^{(j)}}W^{\mathrm{H}}_{Z^{(j)}}\|\|(\Sigma_{Z^{(j)}}W^{\mathrm{H}}_{Z^{(j)}})^{-1}\|=\frac{\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}Z^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(Z^{(j)})}.

(E.3)

Using the definition of
Z^{(j)}
, the norm in the numerator above can be estimated as

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}Z^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\|+\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\|+\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+\mu\|\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|.

Using again the definition of
Z^{(j)}
and Weyl’s inequality, the denominator in (
E.3
) can be estimated from below as follows

\displaystyle\sigma_{\text{min}}(Z^{(j)})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}})-\mu\|\left(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\right)V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle\geq 1-\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\geq 1-\mu\|\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\geq 1-\mu(\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|+\|(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\mu\Big(\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\mu\Big(\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|+10\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\Big)\geq\frac{1}{2},

where the last inequality follows from the assumption on
\mu
.
Now, we can come back to the estimation of
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|
, which due to the combination of the above-carried estimated reads as

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq 2\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+2\mu\|\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

providing the first result from the Lemma. The second bound stated in the Lemma follows from our assumption on
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|
and
\mu
and the fact that the constant
c
is
chosen small enough to make
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{1}{50}
.

∎

Lemma E.3
.

Assume that
{\mu\leq c_{1}\min\left\{\tfrac{1}{10}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2},\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|^{-1}\right\}}
and
{\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}
.
Moreover, suppose that tensor
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
has all invertible t-SVD-tubes and that
{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq c_{1}\kappa^{-1}}
, with absolute constant
c_{1}>0
chosen small enough.
Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(1-\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|^{2}+9\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

for each
j
, with
1\leq j\leq k
.

Proof.

First, we will consider tensor
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}
splitting it into two different parts, and then will conduct the corresponding norm estimations of each Fourier slices.

To begin with, note that for the tensor-column space of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
, that is
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}
, it holds that
{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}+\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}=\bm{\mathcal{I}}}
(see, for example,
[
26
]
).
Using this, we can represent
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}
as follows

\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}+\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}

(E.4)

where the last equality follows from Lemma
C.1
due to the property
{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}=0}
.

Now, we split the term
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}
into two parts using
{\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}+\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}=\bm{\mathcal{I}}}
, which leads to

\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}+\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}

(E.5)

To estimate the norm of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}
in each slice in the Fourier domain, we will use the above-given representation and estimate each of the summands individually.
Let us start with the second one. Its
j
th slice in the Fourier domain reads as

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu{(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp})}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)},\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{{(j)}}.

Due to the orthogonality of the columns of
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}}
, it holds that
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)},\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{{(j)}}\|=\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)},\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{{(j)}}\|
. In the Fourier domain, this allows us to focus on
j
th slices of the last one

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)},\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{{(j)}}:=G_{2}^{(j)}.

Due to the definition of the gradient descent iterates
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}
, we have the following representation for its blocks
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}
in the Fourier domain

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}=\Big(\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}\Big)\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}

To upper bound the norm of
G_{2}^{(j)}
, we want to apply Lemma
H.3
.
Due to the assumptions in this lemma that
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
has full tubal rank with all invertible t-SVD-tubes and
{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq c\kappa^{-1}}
in addition to the conditions on
\mu
and the decomposition of gradient descent iterates into the signal and noise term, the conditions of Lemma
H.3

are satisfied for the choice
{Y_{1}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}
and
{Y=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}
and
Z
as
{Z=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}}
. This allows to upper-bound the norm of
G_{2}^{(j)}
as follows

\displaystyle\|G_{2}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|\Big(1-\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|^{2}+\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|\Big)

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\Big(\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}+\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|^{3}

Using now the fact that for each
j
it holds that

\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|\leq\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

and that
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
, we can proceed with the bound for the norm of
G_{2}^{(j)}
as below

\displaystyle\|G_{2}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|\Big(1-\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|^{2}+\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\Big(9\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|^{3}

Further, using the assumption
\mu\leq c_{1}\min\left\{\tfrac{1}{10}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2},\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|^{-1}\right\}
, we get

\displaystyle\|G_{2}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|\Big(1-\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|^{2}+\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)+\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|^{3}

\displaystyle=\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|\Big(1-\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|^{2}+\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big).

Now, let us return to the first summand in (
E.5
), that is
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}
.
Using again the fact that
{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}=0}

allows us to rewrite it as

\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}=-\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}

(E.6)

Moreover, for the same summand, the corresponding
j
th slice in the Fourier domain reads as

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{(j)}:=G_{1}^{(j)}.

Due to relation (
E.6
) in the tensor domain, in the Fourier domain it holds that

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{(j)}=-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{(j)},

which allows to represent
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{(j)}
as

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{(j)}=-\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{(j)}.

Note that the matrix on the RHS above is invertible due to the assumption that
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
has full tubal rank with all invertible t-SVD-tubes. From here,
G_{1}^{(j)}
can be represented as

G_{1}^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1,\perp}^{(j)}.

According to Lemma
H.3
, the norm of
G_{1}^{(j)}
can be bounded from above as

\displaystyle\|G_{1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu\Big(\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}+\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|\Big)\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu\Big(\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}+\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)\cdot\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu\Big(\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}+\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)\cdot\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|

Due to
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq\frac{1}{50}
from Lemma
E.2
, the fact that
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|
, and our assumption that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
, the norm of
G_{1}^{(j)}
can be further bounded as

\displaystyle\|G_{1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\Big(9\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle=\mu\Big(9\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|.

Since due to representation (
E.4
), it holds that

\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|=\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|,

combining the inequalities for
\|G_{1}^{(j)}\|
and
\|G_{2}^{(j)}\|
together with
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
leads to the final result

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(1-\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|^{2}+9\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+2\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\big)\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|.

∎

The next lemma shows that the tensors
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
and
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}
span approximately
the same tensor column space.

Lemma E.4
.

Assume that the following conditions hold

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|,

(E.7)

\displaystyle\mu

\displaystyle\leq c\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\kappa^{-2}

(E.8)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq c\kappa^{-1}

(E.9)

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}),

(E.10)

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu).

(E.11)

Then it holds that

\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}\|\leq\mu\Big(\tfrac{1}{4800}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|\Big)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+4\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

and
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\geq\frac{1}{2}
,
1\leq j\leq k
.

Proof.

To bound the norm of
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}
, we will rewrite
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}
in the Fourier domain with the help of Fourier slices of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}^{\top}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}
. First, note that due to the decomposition of the gradient iterates into the noise and signal term, it holds
\bm{\mathcal{V}}^{\top}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}=\bm{\mathcal{V}}^{\top}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}^{\top}
.
This allows us to represent the corresponding
j
th Fourier slices of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}^{\top}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}

as
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}
, which means that for each
j
, the matrices
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}
and
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}
have the same kernel, and therefore
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}
spans the same subspace as
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}
. Due to this and the following representation of the matrices

\displaystyle\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}}

\displaystyle=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}+\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}

(E.12)

\displaystyle\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}}

\displaystyle=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}+\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}},

(E.13)

we can apply Lemma
H.4
to estimate
the norm of
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}
taking
{Y_{1}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}
and
{Y=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}
and
Z
as

{Z^{(j)}:=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}}.

This gives us the following estimate

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\left(1+\mu\frac{\|Z^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)})}\right)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|

(E.14)

\displaystyle+\mu\frac{\|Z^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)})}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|.

To proceed further with the upper bound above, we will first show that in each Fourier slice it holds that

\sigma_{\text{min}}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\big)\geq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}),\quad 1\leq j\leq k.

(E.15)

First, note that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\text{min}}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\big)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\big)=\sigma_{\text{min}}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}(\mathrm{Id}+\mu Z^{(j)})\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\big)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\text{min}}\big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}(\mathrm{Id}+\mu Z^{(j)})V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\big)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}(\mathrm{Id}+\mu Z^{(j)})V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\Big)\cdot\sigma_{\text{min}}\big(V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\big)

\displaystyle\geq\bigg(\sigma_{\text{min}}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\Big)-\mu\big\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}Z^{(j)}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\big\|\bigg)\cdot\sigma_{\text{min}}\big(V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\big).

Due to our assumption (
E.9
) on the principal angle
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|
and the properties of the tensor slices, we have that

\sigma_{\text{min}}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\Big)\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\Big)=\sqrt{1-\Big\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\Big\|^{2}}\geq\frac{3}{4},

where that last inequality can be guaranteed by choosing
c>0
small enough.
Thus, to show that relation (
E.15
) holds we need to demonstrate that
\mu\big\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}Z^{(j)}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\big\|
be bounded from above by
\frac{1}{4}
. For this, we will proceed as follows

\displaystyle\mu\big\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}Z^{(j)}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\big\|\leq\mu\big\|Z^{(j)}\big\|\leq\mu\big\|Z^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\big\|+\mu\big\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\|.

(E.16)

By the definition of
Z^{(j)}
, for the first summand from above we have

\displaystyle\Big\|Z^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\Big\|

\displaystyle=\Big\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\Big\|

\displaystyle=\Big\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\big)(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\big(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\big)(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\Big\|

and for the second summand, it holds that

\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2}.

This allows us to proceed with inequality (
E.16
) as

\displaystyle\mu\big\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}Z^{(j)}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\big\|\big(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\big)(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\big\|+\mu(\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\mu\big\|\big(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\big)(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\big\|+10\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2})\leq\mu c\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+11\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2},

where in the first line we used assumption (
E.7
), and in the second assumption(
E.11
). The third inequality above follows from our assumption on
\mu
and sufficiently small constant
c>0
. This, in turn, shows that relation (
E.15
) holds and we can proceed with (
E.14
) in the following manner

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\left(1+2\mu\frac{\|Z^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)})}\right)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle+2\mu\frac{\|Z^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)})}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|.

Now, using assumption (
E.10
) and the definition of
Z^{(j)}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle+4\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|

\displaystyle+4\mu^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\big(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\big)^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle+4\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle+4\mu^{2}\|\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|.

In the last inequality, we used the tensor norm as the maximum norm in each Fourier slice.
Note that, similarly to one of the estimates above, we get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|+\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2}+c\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\leq 11\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

(E.17)

where the last line holds due to the assumption
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
and that
c
is small enough.

Now, since
\mu\leq c\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\kappa^{-2}
,
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
and
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
, constant
c>0
can be chosen so that
{4\mu\cdot 11\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{4800}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}
, together with (
E.17
) and (
E.11
) we can proceed with the estimation of
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}
as

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\left(\tfrac{1}{4800}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+9\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\right)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|+4\mu c\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu).

Using the assumption
\mu\leq c\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}
and choosing
c>0
small enough, we obtain that
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|\leq\frac{1}{2}
. Note that this implies
that
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})=\sqrt{1-\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|^{2}}\geq\frac{1}{2}
, which finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma E.5
.

Assume that the following conditions hold

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}),

(E.18)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|,

(E.19)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{c}

(E.20)

\displaystyle\mu

\displaystyle\leq c\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\kappa^{-2}

(E.21)

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|

\displaystyle\leq c\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

(E.22)

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma_{min}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu).

(E.23)

Then the angle between the column space of the signal term
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
and
column space of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
stays sufficiently small from one iteration to another, namely

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\right)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle+150\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|+500\mu^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}.

Proof.

To estimate the principal angle
\|{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}}}\|
, we first investigate the tensor-column subspace of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}
. By the definition of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}
and
{\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}+\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}=\mathcal{I}}
,
we have

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\Big(\bm{\mathcal{I}}+\mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\Big)*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\mathcal{I}}+\mu\bm{\mathcal{Z}})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}+(\bm{\mathcal{I}}+\mu\bm{\mathcal{Z}})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}.

where we use notation
\bm{\mathcal{Z}}:=(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}).
This allows to represent
j
th slice of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}
in the Fourier domain as

\displaystyle\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}+(\mathrm{Id}+\mu\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}.

with
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muZ\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}
. Because of this representation and decomposition (
E.12
), to bound the principal angle between
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}
and
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
, we want to apply inequality (
H.5
) from Lemma
H.4
, but for this we first need to check whether for

P^{(j)}:=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\Big(V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}

the following applies

\|\mu Z^{(j)}+P^{(j)}+\mu Z^{(j)}P^{(j)}\|\leq 1.

For convenience, we denote
B^{(j)}:=\mu Z^{(j)}+P^{(j)}+\mu Z^{(j)}P^{(j)}
. Using the triangular inequality and submultiplicativity of the norm, we bet the first simple bound on the norm of
B^{(j)}

\|B^{(j)}\|\leq\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|+(1+\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|)\|P^{(j)}\|

(E.24)

Note that
P^{(j)}
can be rewritten as

P^{(j)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}\Big(V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}},

which allows for the following estimate of its norm

\displaystyle\|P^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t,\perp}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|\Big\|\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}\Big\|\Big\|\Big(V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}\Big\|\|V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}^{\mathrm{H}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t,\perp}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)})\cdot\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)})}.

From here, using assumption (
E.18
) and a lower bound on
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)})
from Lemma
E.4
, we get

\|P^{(j)}\|\leq 4\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|.

(E.25)

Using this and the definition of
Z^{(j)}
, we have

\|B^{(j)}\|\leq\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|+4\Big(1+\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|.

(E.26)

Due to the assumption on
\mu
, we can bound
\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|
as follows

\displaystyle\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu(c\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+10\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2})\leq 1

where in the two last inequalities we use assumptions (
E.23
), (
E.19
) and (
E.21
) with the fact for the learning rate constant
c>0
can be chosen sufficiently small.

This, in turn, allows us to proceed with inequality (
E.26
) as

\|B^{(j)}\|\leq\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|+8\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|.

(E.27)

Now, applying the bound on
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}^{\top}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}\|
from Lemma
E.4
and similar transformation for
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|
as above, we come the following result in (
E.27
)

\displaystyle\|B^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|+\mu\Big(\tfrac{1}{600}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)^{2}+8\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|\Big)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle+33\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

To show that this bound above can be made smaller than one, we use assumptions (
E.22
), (
E.23
) and that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|\leq 2\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
, which leads to

\displaystyle\|B^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|+\mu\Big(\tfrac{1}{600}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)^{2}+8c\frac{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{\kappa^{2}}\cdot 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|\Big)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+33\mu c\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

\displaystyle\leq\mu 10\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\mu c\frac{1}{300}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+33\mu c\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\leq 1,

with the last inequality following from the assumption on
\mu
. In such a way, we check the conditions of Lemma
H.4
to be able to apply inequality (
H.5
). This gives

\displaystyle\|V_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|V_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\left(1-\frac{\mu}{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})+\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\|Z^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|+\left(1+\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|\right)\frac{2\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)})\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)})}
