\displaystyle+57\left(\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|+(1+\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|)\frac{\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t,\perp}^{(j)}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)})\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)})}\right)^{2}.

Applying again assumption (
E.18
) and a lower bound on
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)})
from Lemma
E.4
as for (
E.25
), in addition to (
E.22
), we get

\displaystyle\|V_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|V_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\left(1-\frac{\mu}{3}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)})\right)+\mu\|Z^{(j)}-(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}})\|

\displaystyle+8\big(1+\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|\big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|+57\left(\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|+4\big(1+\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|\big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|\right)^{2}.

Now, making
\big(1+\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|\big)\leq 3
by choosing
c>0
small enough and using the properties of the terms involved, the above inequality gets the following view

\displaystyle\|V_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|V_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}}\|\left(1-\frac{\mu}{3}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\right)+\mu\|(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle+32\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|+57\left(\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|+12\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|\right)^{2}.

(E.28)

To proceed further with (
E.28
), we will first do several auxiliary estimates. We start by bounding the norm
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|
. Since it holds that
\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}\|
, from Lemma
E.4
, one gets

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\Big(\tfrac{1}{4800}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|\Big)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle+4\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\big(\tfrac{1}{4800}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+3c\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\big)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+4\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{2400}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+4\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

(E.29)

where we use in the second inequality that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
and
\|{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|\leq c\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
by assumption, and in the last line that
c>0
can be chosen small enough. Using this estimate, let us bound from above the squared term in (
E.28
) as follows

\displaystyle\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|+12\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|+\mu\frac{\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{200}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+48\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t}\|+\mu\frac{\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{200}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle+49\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|.

From here, using Jensen’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle(\mu\|Z^{(j)}\|+12\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t,\perp}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\|)^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 3\mu^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t}\|^{2}+3\mu^{2}\frac{\sigma_{\text{min}}^{4}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{200^{2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\cdot 49^{2}\mu^{2}\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|^{2}.

Now, we can come back to bounding (
E.28
) proceeding as follows

\displaystyle\|V_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\left(1-\frac{\mu}{3}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+\frac{4\mu}{300}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\right)

\displaystyle+129\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle+171\mu^{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muX\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}_{t}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}_{t}\|^{2}+\mu^{2}\frac{171\sigma_{\text{min}}^{4}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{200^{2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+171\cdot 49^{2}\mu^{2}\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\left(1-\frac{\mu}{3}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+\frac{4\mu}{300}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)+\frac{171}{200^{2}}\kappa^{-4}\widetilde{c}\cdot c\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\right)

\displaystyle+171\mu^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\top}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mu(129+171\cdot 49^{2}c^{2}\kappa^{-4})\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|,

where for the last inequality we used assumptions (
E.23
), (
E.20
) and (
E.21
), and the properties of the tubal tensor norm. Now choosing constant
c>0
sufficiently small, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|V_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}V_{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t+1}^{(j)}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\right)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+200\mu^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\top}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+150\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|.

Since the right-hand side of the above inequality is independent of
j
, we obtain the lemma statement.
∎

The following lemma shows that under a mild condition the technical assumption

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

needed in the lemmas above holds.

Lemma E.6
.

Assume that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
,
\mu\leq\tfrac{1}{27}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}
and that linear measurement operator
\mathcal{A}
is such that

\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

Then for the iteration
t+1
, it also holds
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
.

Proof.

Consider the gradient iterate

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}+\mu\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}+\mu(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}+\mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\mathcal{I}}-\mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}+\mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}+\mu(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}.

To estimate the norm of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}
, we will bound each summand above separately. Due to the assumption on
\mu
and the norm of
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}
, we have
\mu\leq\tfrac{1}{27}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\leq\tfrac{1}{3}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{-2}
. This allows us to estimate the tensor norm of
(\bm{\mathcal{I}}-\mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}
via the norm of matrix block representation in the Fourier domain. Namely, assume that matrix
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu
has the SVD
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu=V\Sigma W^{\mathrm{H}}
. Then for matrix
\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\bm{\mathcal{I}}-\mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu
, we have

\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\bm{\mathcal{I}}-\mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu=V\Sigma W^{\mathrm{H}}-\mu V\Sigma W^{\mathrm{H}}W\Sigma V^{\mathrm{H}}V\Sigma W^{\mathrm{H}}=V\Sigma W^{\mathrm{H}}-\mu V\Sigma^{3}W^{\mathrm{H}}=V(\Sigma-\mu\Sigma^{3})W^{\mathrm{H}}.

From here, since
{\mu\leq\tfrac{1}{27}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\leq\tfrac{1}{3}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{-2}}
and
{\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|=\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\|}
, it holds that
{\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu(\bm{\mathcal{I}}-\mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\|=\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\|-\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu\|^{3}=\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|(1-\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2})}
. Besides,
from the submultiplicativity of the tensor norm and the triangle
inequality, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\|

\displaystyle\leq(1-\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2}+\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|

(E.30)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2})\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|,

(E.31)

where in the last line we used the assumption on
\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|
.
By combining inequality (
E.31
) with the assumption
\mu\leq\frac{1}{27\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{3\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|^{2}}
, we obtain that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

, which finishes the proof.
∎

The following lemma shows that
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
converges towards

\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{T}
, when projected onto the tensor column space of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
.

Lemma E.7
.

Assume that the following conditions hold

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

(E.32)

\displaystyle\mu

\displaystyle\leq c\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{nk}}\cdot\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}

(E.33)

\displaystyle\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)

(E.34)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|

\displaystyle\leq c\kappa^{-2}

(E.35)

and

\displaystyle\max\Big\{\big\|

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})\big\|_{F},\;\big\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})\big\|_{F},\;\big\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{Y}}_{t})\big\|\Big\}\leq\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}\|_{F}

with
\bm{\mathcal{Y}}_{t}:=\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
.
Then it holds that

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

(E.36)

as well as

\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}\leq 4\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

(E.37)

and

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\mu}{200}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)\right)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\mu\frac{\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{X}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}{100}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

(E.38)

Proof.

We start by proving the first inequality (
E.38
). For this, let us decompose
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
as follows

\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}+\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top},

then using the triangle inequality and submultiplicativity of the Frobenius and the spectral norm, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F},

(E.39)

where in the second line, we used the orthogonality of the decomposition. Now, we will work additionally on bounding the norm of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}
to obtain (
E.38
).
Here, we will use the orthogonal decomposition with respect to
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
and
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

Now, for the first term above, we get

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}=\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\|\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\left\|\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\Big)^{-1}\right\|\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muW\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muU\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{t}^{{(j)}\mathrm{H}}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muV\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{(j)}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|}{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu)}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}\leq 2\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

where in the last line we used the assumption (
E.35
). Them, using just established bound together with (
E.39
), we get

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}.

To get inequality (
E.37
), we use the orthogonal decomposition of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
with respect to
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}
and
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 4\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}.

Inequality (
E.38
) follows from the two inequalities proved here and Lemma 9.5 in
[
36
]
.
The building stones for this are the properties of the tubal tensor Frobenius norm. Namely, the Frobenius norm of any tubal tensor
\bm{\mathcal{T}}
can be represented as the sum of Frobenius norms of each slice in the domain, that is

\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}\|_{F}=\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|_{F}

and
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{n\cdot k}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}\|.
Besides, the Frobenius norm of the product of two tensors
\bm{\mathcal{T}}
and
\bm{\mathcal{P}}
can be bounded as below

\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}*\bm{\mathcal{P}}\|_{F}=\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muP\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|_{F}\leq\max_{1\leq j\leq k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muT\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5muP\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|_{F}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{P}}\|_{F}.

∎

Now, we have collected all the necessary ingredients to prove the main result of this section, which shows that after a sufficient number of interactions, the relative error between
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
and
\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}
becomes small.

Theorem E.1
.

Suppose that the stepsize satisfies
\mu\leq c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-4}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}
for some small
c_{1}>0
, and
\mathcal{A}:S^{n\times n\times k}\to\mathbb{R}^{m}
satisfies
\text{RIP}(2r+1,\delta)
for some constant
0<\delta\leq\dfrac{c_{1}}{\kappa^{4}\sqrt{r}}
. Set
\gamma\in(0,\tfrac{1}{2})
, and choose a number of iterations
t_{*}
such that
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{*}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{*}})\geq\gamma
. Also, assume that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{*}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{*},\perp}\|\leq 2\gamma
,
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{*}}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
,
\gamma\leq\dfrac{c_{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{\kappa^{2}\min\{n,R\}}
, and
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{*}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{*}}}\|\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2}
for some small
c_{2}>0
. Then, after

\widehat{t}-t_{*}\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\min\left\{1,\dfrac{\kappa r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\right\}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}{\gamma}\right)

additional iterations, we have

\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\|_{F}}{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}\lesssim k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}\gamma^{21/16}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-21/16}.

Proof.

First, we set

t_{1}=\min\left\{t\geq t_{*}:\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\geq\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\overline{\bm{\mathcal{X}}})\right\},

and then aim to prove that over the iterations
t_{*}\leq t\leq t_{1}
, the following hold:

•

\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\geq\tfrac{1}{2}\gamma\left(1+\tfrac{1}{8}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{*}}

•

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|\leq 2\gamma\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{*}}

•

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

•

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2}
.

Intuitively, this means that over the range
t_{*}\leq t\leq t_{1}
, the smallest singular value of the signal term
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}
grows at a faster rate than the largest singular value of the noise term
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}
.

For
t=t_{*}
, these inequalities hold due to the assumptions of this theorem. Now, suppose they hold for some
t
between
t_{*}
and
t_{1}
. We’ll show they also hold for
t+1
.

First, note that we have:

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|+\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle(a)\hskip 7.22743pt\leq

\displaystyle\delta\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|+\delta\sqrt{k}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta\sqrt{kr}\left(\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|\right)+\delta\sqrt{k}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta\sqrt{kr}\left(\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|^{2}\right)+\delta\sqrt{k}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta\sqrt{kr}\left(\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2}\right)+\delta\sqrt{k}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{*}

\displaystyle(b)\hskip 7.22743pt\leq

\displaystyle\delta\sqrt{kr}\left(\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+9\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\right)+\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10\delta\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10\delta\sqrt{kr}\kappa^{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|^{2}

\displaystyle(c)\hskip 7.22743pt\leq

\displaystyle 10c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+4\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\gamma^{2}\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t-t_{*})}

\displaystyle(d)\hskip 7.22743pt\leq

\displaystyle 10c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+8\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\gamma^{7/4}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/4}

\displaystyle(e)\hskip 7.22743pt\leq

\displaystyle 40c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}.

In inequality (a), we used the fact that
\mathcal{A}
satisfies
\text{RIP}(2r+1,\delta)
(and hence,
\text{RIP}(r+1,\delta)
and
\text{RIP}(2,\delta)
), and thus, by Lemmas
G.2
and
G.3
, also satisfies
\text{S2SRIP}(r,\delta\sqrt{kr})
and
\text{S2NRIP}(\delta\sqrt{k})
. Inequality (b) uses the assumption
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
and the fact that
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
has tubal rank at most
\min\{n,R\}-r
. In inequality (c), we used the assumption
\delta\leq\dfrac{c_{1}}{\kappa^{4}\sqrt{r}}
along with the second bulleted inequality assumed by the inductive step. Inequality (d) holds due to the definitions of
t_{1}
and
t_{*}
and the fact that
t_{*}\leq t\leq t_{1}
. Finally, inequality (e) holds due to the assumption
\gamma\leq\tfrac{c_{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{\kappa^{2}\min\{n,R\}}
.

If
c_{1}
is chosen small enough, the above bound is less than
\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
. Then, along with our other assumptions, we can use Lemma
E.6
to obtain
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
.

Next, we can use Lemma
E.1
along with the bound
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\leq\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\overline{\bm{\mathcal{X}}})
to obtain

\displaystyle\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\left(1+\dfrac{1}{4}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}-\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\left(1+\dfrac{1}{4}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}-\dfrac{1}{10}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\left(1+\dfrac{1}{8}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\dfrac{1}{2}\gamma\left(1+\dfrac{1}{8}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{*}}\cdot\left(1+\dfrac{1}{8}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{2}\gamma\left(1+\dfrac{1}{8}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{*}+1}

Since
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1})=\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1})
, which is positive by the above bound, all the singular tubes of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}
are invertible. Hence, we can apply Lemma
E.3
to obtain

\displaystyle\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(1-\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|^{2}+9\mu\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(1-\frac{\mu}{2}\cdot 4\gamma^{2}\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t-t_{*})}+9\mu c_{2}\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\mu\cdot 40c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(1-\frac{\mu}{2}\cdot 4\gamma^{2}\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t-t_{*})}+9\mu c_{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+80c_{1}\mu\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(1+80c_{1}\mu\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(1+80c_{1}\mu\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\Big)\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma\Big(1+80c_{1}\mu\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\Big)^{t-t_{*}+1},

where we have used the inductive assumption that the inequalities hold for
t
along with the fact that
\kappa=\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|/\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\geq 1
.

Next, we will bound the term using Lemma
E.5

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}}}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|+150\mu\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|+500\mu^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)c_{2}\kappa^{-2}+150\mu\cdot 40c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+500\mu^{2}\cdot(\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)c_{2}\kappa^{-2}+6000\mu c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+500\mu^{2}\cdot(\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+9\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)c_{2}\kappa^{-2}+6000\mu c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+50000\mu^{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)c_{2}\kappa^{-2}+6000\mu c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+50000\mu\cdot c_{1}\kappa^{-4}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-2}\cdot\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)c_{2}\kappa^{-2}+6000\mu c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+50000\mu\cdot c_{1}\kappa^{-4}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)c_{2}\kappa^{-2}+6000\mu c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+50000\mu\cdot c_{1}\kappa^{-4}\kappa^{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)c_{2}\kappa^{-2}+56000\mu c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}

Here, we have again used the inductive assumptions along with the fact that
\kappa=\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|/\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})
. If we choose
c_{1}
sufficiently small, we will have
\|{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}}}\|\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2}
.

Therefore, the four bullet points hold for
t+1
, and thus, the induction is complete.

With the above bullet points in mind, we note that

\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}})\geq\dfrac{1}{2}\gamma\left(1+\dfrac{1}{8}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t_{1}-t_{*}},

and so,

t_{1}-t_{*}\leq\dfrac{\log\left(\dfrac{2}{\gamma\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)}{\log\left(1+\dfrac{1}{8}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)}\leq\dfrac{16}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\log\left(\dfrac{2}{\gamma\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right),

where we have used the inequality
\tfrac{1}{\log(1+x)}\leq\tfrac{2}{x}
for
0<x<1
. Furthermore, we can bound the norm of the signal term at iteration
t_{1}
by

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{1},\perp}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t_{1}-t_{*}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma\left(\dfrac{2}{\sqrt{10}}\cdot\dfrac{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{\gamma}\right)^{1280c_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma\left(\dfrac{2}{\sqrt{10}}\cdot\dfrac{\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{\gamma}\right)^{1/64}

\displaystyle\leq 3\gamma^{63/64}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/64}

\displaystyle\leq 3\gamma^{7/8}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/8},

where we have used the previous bound on
t_{1}-t_{*}
, the fact that
c_{2}>0
can be chosen to be sufficiently small, and the fact that
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\geq\gamma
.

Next, we set

t_{2}=t_{1}+\left\lfloor\dfrac{300}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right)\right\rfloor

t_{3}=\min\left\{t\geq t_{1}:\left(\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}+1\right)\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\right\|_{F}\geq\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}\right\}

\widehat{t}=\min\{t_{2},t_{3}\}.

We now aim to show that over the range
t_{1}\leq t\leq\widehat{t}
, the following inequalities hold:

•

\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t})\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\geq\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})

•

\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\right\|\leq\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{1}}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{1},\perp}\right\|

•

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

•

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2}

•

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}\leq 10\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\tfrac{1}{400}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

For
t=t_{1}
, the first four bullet points follow from what we previously proved via induction. The last one holds since we trivially have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\|+\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 10\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}.

Now suppose all the bullet points hold for some integer
t\in[t_{1},\widehat{t}-1]
. Again, we aim to show they all hold for
t+1
. In a similar manner as done before, we can bound
\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|\leq 10\delta\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|^{2}
, and then continue as follows

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10\delta\sqrt{kr}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10\cdot\dfrac{c_{1}}{\kappa^{4}\sqrt{r}}\cdot\sqrt{kr}\cdot\kappa^{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t-t_{1})}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{1},\perp}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t-t_{1})}\cdot 9\gamma^{7/4}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+9\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t_{2}-t_{1})}\gamma^{7/4}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}+9\delta\sqrt{k}(\min\{n,R\}-r)\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right)^{O(c_{2})}\gamma^{7/4}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 40c_{1}\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}

where we have used the bounds
\delta\leq\tfrac{c_{1}}{\kappa^{4}\sqrt{r}}
,
\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|=\kappa\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})
,
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{1},\perp}\|\leq 3\gamma^{7/8}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/8}
, along with the inductive assumptions and the definition of
t_{1}
.

Next, we note that if
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})
, then we can use Lemma
E.1
along with the inductive assumptions to obtain

\displaystyle\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\left(1+\dfrac{1}{4}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}-\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\left(1+\dfrac{1}{4}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}-\mu\cdot\dfrac{1}{4}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})

\displaystyle\geq\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})

Alternatively, if
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\geq\tfrac{1}{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})
, then we can again use Lemma
E.1
along with the inductive assumptions and the fact that
\mu\leq c_{1}\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}
for sufficiently small
c_{1}
to obtain

\displaystyle\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\left(1+\dfrac{1}{4}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}-\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\dfrac{1}{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\left(1-\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\dfrac{1}{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\left(1-\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\dfrac{1}{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\left(1-9\mu\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\dfrac{1}{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\left(1-9c_{1}\kappa^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})

In either case, we have
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1})\geq\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1})\geq\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})
.

Again, since
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t})\geq\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})>0
, we have that
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
has full tubal rank with all invertible t-SVD singular tubes. Hence, by Lemma
E.3
, we again can bound

\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1,\perp}\right\|\leq\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t+1-t_{1}}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{1},\perp}\right\|.

In the exact same way as before, we can use Lemma
E.6
to establish
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
, and use Lemma
E.7
to establish
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t+1}}\|\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2}
.

To bound
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}^{\top})\|_{F}
, we will aim to use Lemma
E.7
. By the inductive assumptions, we already have
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\|\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|
,
\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t})\geq\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})
, and
\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}\|\leq c_{2}\kappa^{-2}
. To derive the remaining condition of Lemma
E.7
, we first split

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}.

To bound the first term, we note that
\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
is tubal-symmetric with tubal rank at most
2r
, so we can write it as the sum of two tubal-symmetric tensors
\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{1},\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{2}\in S^{n\times n\times k}
with tubal rank at most
r
, and then apply Lemma
G.4
to obtain

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{1}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{2})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{1})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{2})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\delta(\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{1}\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{2}\|_{F})

\displaystyle\leq\delta\sqrt{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{1}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\delta\sqrt{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\sqrt{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

For the second piece, we use the symmetric t-SVD to write
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}=\sum_{i}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}*\bm{s}_{i}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\top}
. Then, we can bound

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})\left(\sum_{i}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}*\bm{s}_{i}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\top}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})\left(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}*\bm{s}_{i}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\top}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\delta\left\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}*\bm{s}_{i}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\top}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\delta\left\|\bm{s}_{i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\delta\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\right\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F},

where we have used the fact that
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}
has tubal rank
\leq\min\{n,R\}-r
along with the definition of
t_{3}
.

Hence,

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta\sqrt{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}+\delta\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F},

where we have used the assumption that
\delta\leq\tfrac{c_{1}}{\kappa^{4}\sqrt{r}}\leq c\kappa^{-2}.

Similarly, we can bound

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}}^{\top}*(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\|_{F}\leq c\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F},

and

\|(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t})\|\leq c\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}.

Then, by Lemma
E.7
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\mu}{200}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\mu\frac{\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{100}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

By the inductive assumption,

\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}\leq 10\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\tfrac{1}{400}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}.

Also, using the inductive assumption and the bound from the previous part, we can bound

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t-t_{1})}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{1},\perp}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t-t_{1})}\cdot 9\gamma^{7/4}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/4}

Since
t\leq t_{2}
, we have

t-t_{1}\leq t_{2}-t_{1}\leq\dfrac{300}{\mu\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{\min\{n,R\}-r}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right),

and thus,

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(t-t_{1})}\cdot 9\gamma^{7/4}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{5}{2}\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\dfrac{\mu}{400}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}.

Combining these inequalities yields

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t+1}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\mu}{200}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\mu\frac{\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{100}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\mu}{200}\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)\cdot 10\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\dfrac{1}{400}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\mu\frac{\sigma_{\text{min}}^{2}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{100}\cdot\dfrac{5}{2}\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\dfrac{\mu}{400}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 10\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\dfrac{1}{400}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{t+1-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

Hence, by induction, the five bullet points hold for
t+1
.

If
\widehat{t}=t_{2}
, then, we can use Lemma
E.7
, the previous bullet points, and the definition of
t_{2}
to bound

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 4\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 40\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\dfrac{1}{400}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{\widehat{t}-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}+\dfrac{5}{2}\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\dfrac{1}{400}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{\widehat{t}-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{85}{2}\sqrt{kr}\left(1-\dfrac{1}{400}\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{\widehat{t}-t_{1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{kr}\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right)^{-3/4}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}\gamma^{21/16}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{11/16}

If instead we have
\widehat{t}=t_{3}
, then

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}^{\top}*(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top})\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}+1)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4(\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}+5)\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}+5)\sqrt{\min\{n,R\}-r}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}^{\top}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}+5)\sqrt{\min\{n,R\}-r}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\widehat{t},\perp}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}+5)\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(\widehat{t}-t_{1})}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{1}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{1},\perp}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}+5)\sqrt{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\left(1+80\mu c_{2}\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}\right)^{2(\widehat{t}-t_{1})}\cdot 9\gamma^{63/32}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/32}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k(\min\{n,R\}-r)\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right)^{O(c_{2})}\gamma^{63/32}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{1/32}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k(\min\{n,R\}-r)\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right)^{O(c_{2})}\gamma^{21/16}\gamma^{21/32}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{1/32}}{\kappa^{1/32}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k(\min\{n,R\}-r)\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right)^{O(c_{2})}\gamma^{21/16}\left(\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}{\min\{n,R\}\kappa^{3}}\right)^{21/32}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{1/32}}{\kappa^{1/32}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}{\min\{n,R\}^{21/32}}\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right)^{O(c_{2})}\gamma^{21/16}\kappa^{-2}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{11/16}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}\gamma^{21/16}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{11/16}.

So in either case, we have

\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}\lesssim k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}\gamma^{21/16}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{11/16},

and thus,

\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}\|_{F}}{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}\lesssim k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}\gamma^{21/16}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-21/16}.

Finally, by the definition of
\widehat{t}
, we have that

\displaystyle\widehat{t}-t_{*}

\displaystyle\leq t_{2}-t_{*}

\displaystyle\leq(t_{2}-t_{1})+(t_{1}-t_{*})

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{300}{\mu\sqrt{k}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\dfrac{5}{18}\kappa^{1/4}\sqrt{\dfrac{r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{7/4}}{\gamma^{7/4}}\right)+\dfrac{16}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\log\left(\dfrac{2}{\gamma\sqrt{10}}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\min\left\{1,\dfrac{\kappa r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\right\}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}{\gamma}\right)

∎

Appendix F
Proof of Main Result

Now that our analyses of the spectral stage and the convergence stage are complete, we are ready to combine these pieces to obtain the proof of our main result. Since
\mathcal{A}
satisfies
\text{RIP}(2r+1,\delta)
, by Lemma
G.2
,
\mathcal{A}
also satisfies
\text{S2SRIP}(2r,\sqrt{2kr}\delta)
. Hence,
\bm{\mathcal{E}}:=(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top})
satisfies

\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}\|=\|(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top})\|\leq\sqrt{2kr}\delta\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\|\leq\sqrt{2kr}\cdot c\kappa^{-4}r^{-1/2}\cdot\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}=c\sqrt{k}\kappa^{-2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}.

Then, by applying Lemma
D.9
, with
\epsilon=\tfrac{1}{\tilde{C}}e^{-3\tilde{c}}
, we have that with probability at least
1-k(\tilde{C}\epsilon)^{R-2r+1}-ke^{\tilde{c}r}\geq 1-ke^{-3\tilde{c}(R-2r+1)}-ke^{\tilde{c}r}\geq 1-ke^{-3\tilde{c}(3r-2r+1)}-ke^{\tilde{c}r}=1-O(ke^{-\tilde{c}r})
, after

t_{*}\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\frac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\tilde{c}_{3}\sqrt{\min\{n;R\}}}\right)

iterations, we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}\|

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|

(F.1)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\perp}}*\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star}}}\|

\displaystyle\leq c\kappa^{-2}.

(F.2)

and for each
1\leq j\leq k
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star}}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4}\alpha\beta

(F.3)

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\Big(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t_{\star}}*\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t_{\star},\perp}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\beta

(F.4)

where (since
R\geq 3r
and
\epsilon
is a constant),

\sqrt{k}\lesssim\beta\lesssim\sqrt{k}\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\tilde{c}_{3}\sqrt{\min\{n;R\}}}\right)^{16\kappa^{2}}.

By choosing

\alpha\lesssim\dfrac{4c_{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{\kappa^{2}\min\{n,R\}\sqrt{k}}\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\tilde{c}_{3}\sqrt{\min\{n,R\}}}\right)^{-16\kappa^{2}},

we have

\gamma=\dfrac{1}{4}\alpha\beta\lesssim\dfrac{c_{2}\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})}{\kappa^{2}\min\{n,R\}}.

Also,
\tfrac{\kappa^{-2}}{8}\alpha\beta=\tfrac{1}{2\kappa^{2}}\gamma\leq 2\gamma
holds. Therefore, we can apply Theorem
E.1
, which gives us that after

\widehat{t}-t_{*}\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\min\left\{1,\dfrac{\kappa r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\right\}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}{\gamma}\right)

iterations beyond the first phase, we have

\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\|_{F}}{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}\lesssim k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}\gamma^{21/16}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-21/16}.

The total amount of iterations is then bounded by

\displaystyle\widehat{t}

\displaystyle=t_{*}+(\widehat{t}-t_{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\tilde{c}_{3}\sqrt{\min\{n,R\}}}\right)+\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\min\left\{1,\dfrac{\kappa r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\right\}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}{\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\tilde{c}_{3}\sqrt{\min\{n,R\}}}\cdot\min\left\{1,\dfrac{\kappa r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\right\}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}{\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\tilde{c}_{3}\sqrt{\min\{n,R\}}}\cdot\min\left\{1,\dfrac{\kappa r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\right\}\dfrac{4\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\mu\sigma_{\text{min}}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{2}}\ln\left(\dfrac{C_{1}\kappa n}{\min\{n,R\}}\cdot\min\left\{1,\dfrac{\kappa r}{k(\min\{n,R\}-r)}\right\}\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}{k\alpha}\right),

where we have used the choice of
\gamma=\tfrac{1}{4}\alpha\beta
and the fact that
\beta\gtrsim\sqrt{k}
. Finally, the error is bounded by

\displaystyle\dfrac{\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}*\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{\widehat{t}}^{\top}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}*\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\top}\|_{F}}{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}\gamma^{21/16}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-21/16}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}(\alpha\beta)^{21/16}\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|^{-21/16}

\displaystyle\lesssim k^{5/4}r^{1/8}\kappa^{-3/16}(\min\{n,R\}-r)^{3/8}k^{21/32}\left(\dfrac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\tilde{c}_{3}\sqrt{\min\{n,R\}}}\right)^{21\kappa^{2}}\left(\dfrac{\alpha}{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|}\right)^{21/16}
